Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the rna structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 312. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 161 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 312 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the rna structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 161. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on rna structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 784. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 809 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on rna structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 784. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 809. QED.
